See Deu. 18:18. 1 “Echad” means “one.” Deu. 6:4: “Hear o Yisrael, YHWH is our Elohim; YHWH is echad!” However, the Hebrew word ‘echad’ does not necessarily mean that it is only one object, or only one person. It often rather has the concept of being united – the opposite of being divided. It is impossible to use the above verse to deny that YHWH has a Son who is indeed Elohim himself – already in Gen. 2: 2 the word ‘echad’ is used of two people becoming one flesh: “Therefore a man will leave his father and his mother, and cleave unto his wife; and they will become one flesh.” See also Mat. 19: 4-6. 2 Hebrew name for ‘Levites.’ 3 Hebrew name for ‘Jerusalem.’ 4 Hebrew name for ‘Elijah.’ 5 See Deu. 18: 15-22. When ‘Prophet’ is used as a name, as in this case, it is used as a synonym of ‘Mashiach.’ (See glossary: Mashiach.) The use of ‘Prophet’ as a name for Mashiach, should be distinguished from the general use of ‘prophet’ for humans. prepare the way for Mashiach. 6 The Hebrew manuscript has “Hashem,” which literally means “The Name.” Still today many Jews will read ‘Hashem’ when they see the Hebrew יהוה. We have replaced “Hashem” with transliterated consonants of ‘the Name’ itself: YHWH. As for pronunciation, the only grammatically possible pronunciation, from which all other abbreviations / contractions can be formed, is ‘Yah-weh.’ Perushim.)1 2 So they asked him, and said to him, “Why are you immersing, if you are not Mashiach, nor Eliyahu, nor Prophet?” 2 water; however, he whom you do not recognize abides among you.

John 1:40	(Now Andrai was the brother of Shimon; and Shimon Keipha was one of them twelve, who heard of 1 Hebrew word for ‘spirit’ or ‘wind.’ 2 Lit. ‘the Set-Apart Spirit.’ 3 Usually translated as “disciples.” The Hebrew “Talmid” is a student who learns from his teacher and follows his example. “Talmidim” is the masculine plural form. 4 Hebrew: “Mal’ach.” Used for both human and heavenly messengers. The English word ‘angel’ is not a translation – only a transliteration of the Greek ‘aggelos.’ Nowhere in the Tanach are heavenly messengers described as ‘women with wings’, as Catholic images and paintings represent them. They are actually YHWH’s “armies” of “powerful warriors” going and doing whatever he sends them for. Ps. 103: 20,2 “Bless YHWH, his messengers, powerful warriors, who perform his word, listening to the voice of his word. Bless YHWH, all his armies, his servants who perform his goodwill.” The phrase ‘Mal’ach YHWH’ (The Messenger of YHWH ) is also often used of Mashiach (YHWH the Son) in the Tanach. No man saw YHWH the Father at any time, and thus every time YHWH appeared to (lit. “was seen of”) someone in the Tanach, he did so through YHWH the Son. John 16:22	And truth, so is your anxiousness; but I will see you again, and your heart will be glad, and no man will take away your joy from you.

John 17:8	For the words which you gave me, I gave to them – and they received them, and they know in truth that I came out from you, and they believe that you sent me.
John 17:9	I pray for them, not for the world – only for these which you gave to me, for they are yours.
John 17:10	And all mine are yours, and yours are mine – and I am glorified in them.
John 17:11	And I am not in the world anymore, but they are in the world; and I am coming unto you. Set- apart Father, keep them in your name – those whom you gave to me – that they may be echad as we are.
John 17:12	While I abode with them, I kept them in your name. Those whom you gave me, I have kept – and none of them is missing, except the son of destruction – in order that the Scripture may be fulfilled.
John 17:13	But now I am coming to you, and speak these words in the world in order that they may have my [joy] fulfilled in [themselves].
John 17:14	I have given them your words; and the world had a report against them.
John 17:15	I do not plead that you will take them from the world, but that you will keep them from evil.
John 17:16	They are not of the world, as I am not of the world.
John 17:17	Sanctify them in truth – your word is truth.
John 17:18	As you sent me in the world, so I send it in the world.

If this phrase was written in the original version of John, it would contradict Mark 15:2 which states that Yeshua was crucified at the third hour (i.e. 9 am). If this comment was inserted by a later editor who used the Roman timing system, ‘the sixth hour’ would mean 6 am, and that is probably the intended meaning. Also, it was not the sixth day of the week, but rather the sixth day in John’s detailed description of Yeshua’s to the Yehudim, “Behold your King!”

John 21:8	But the remainder of the talmidim came with boats, for they were not far from the land, only about two hundred cubits. 1 Or possibly “at the sea of.” 2 This is the Hebrew name for ‘Didymus,’ and means “doubting” or “doubtful.” 3 The Hebrew name for ‘Zebedee.’ Could also be vocalized as “Zavdi.” 4 Lit. “place,” but often has the connotation of ‘cast.’ 5 Could also mean “on the other side.” 6 Lit. “placed,” but often has the connotation of “cast.” 7 Often refers to outer clothing.
John 21:9	So when they went down to the land, they saw a prepared fire, with burning coals ready, and fish set upon it, and bread.
John 21:10	Then Yeshua said to them, “Bring some of the fish which you caught just now.”
John 21:11	So Shimon Keipha went up into the boat, and brought the nets to the land, filled with an abundance of fish – hundred and fifty three by number! And with all this – that the fish were so many, the net did not break.
John 21:12	Then Yeshua said to them, “Come and eat.” (Now none of them wanted to ask who he was, for they knew that he was their Adon.)
John 21:13	So Yeshua came, and took the bread and the fish, and gave to them.
John 21:14	(And by then it was three times that Yeshua had revealed himself to his talmidim after he returned to life.)
John 21:15	Then, as he ate, Yeshua said to Shimon Keipha, “Shimon of [Yonah], do you love me more than these?” Then he answered, “Adon, yes – you know that I love you.” So Yeshua said to them, “Keep my lambs.”
John 21:16	Then Yeshua asked him again, “Shimon Keipha, son of [Yonah], do you love me?” So Shimon said, “Yes Adon, you know that I love you.” Then Yeshua said unto him, “Shepherd and feed my lambs.”
John 21:17	He asked him a third time, “Shimon of [Yonah], do you love me?” (Now Keipha was anxious because he had asked him three times if he loved him.) He said to him, “Adon knowing all things, you know that I love you!” So Yeshua said to him, “Feed my flock.
John 21:18	Truth I say to you – when I was a young man you were staying and going to the place of your pleasure, but when you become an elder you will stretch out your hand and another will go after you 1 Or “child.” and drive you into a place that you do not find pleasure in.”
John 21:19	(And Yeshua said [this], alluding with what death he would glorify El.) And when he had said this, he said to Keipha, “Come after me!”
John 21:20	Then Keipha turned himself back, and saw that talmid whom Yeshua loved exceedingly, going after him – he who reclined on his chest at the meal.
John 21:21	So when Keipha saw this, he asked Yeshua, “What will become of this man?”
John 21:22	So Yeshua said unto him, “I want that he abides here until I will come – what is it to you? You, come after me!”
John 21:23	Therefore this word went out among his brothers that this talmid would not die; but he only said “I want that he abides here until I will come – what is it to you?”
John 21:24	This is the talmid who does witness of these things, and wrote these things, and you know that his witness is true.
John 21:25	And many more things Yeshua did, that if all of them should be written – I think – all the world would not be able to contain the scrolls which would be needed in their writing. Complete and sealed. Blessed is he who set me free. 1 Or “signifying.” 2 Or possibly ‘sustain.’ Joh. 1 Glossary Adon Standard Hebrew word for ‘Master.’ ‘Adon’ or ‘Ha-Adon’ is often used for YHWH in quotes from the Old Testament. Andrai Hebrew name for ‘Andrew.’ Avraham Hebrew name for ‘Abraham.’ Bar-Even Bar-Even means ‘Son of a Stone.’ Hebrew could also be read as “Bar-Ravan,” meaning ‘Son of a Teacher.’ Not the same meaning as the Greek (from Aramaic) ‘Barabba(s),’ which means ‘Son of the Father.’ Beit-Anyah Hebrew name for ‘Bethany.’ Beit-Gelilah Lit. “the house of Galilee.” Beit-Lechem Lit. “house of bread.” Hebrew name for ‘Bethlehem.’ Dawid Hebrew name for ‘David.’ Echad Hebrew word for ‘one,’ in the Tanach it often has the meaning ‘united.’ El Usually translated as ‘God.’ In Hebrew however, the word ‘luck’ or ‘fortune’ is pronounced as “gad” and sometimes as “god” (medieval) – see e.g. Gen. 30:10-11; Josh. 11:17; Isa. 65:11. Therefore we prefer to not use ‘God.’ Eloah Same meaning as El, see above note. Elohim Plural form of Eloah, see above note. Glossary Eliezer Hebrew name for ‘Lazarus.’ Possible shorter form is “Lazar.” Eliyahu Hebrew name for ‘Elijah.’ Gelilah Hebrew name for ‘Galilee.’ Ha-Satan Lit. ‘The Adversary.’ Because it is often used as a name in these gospels (See e.g. Mat. 4:10) we have transliterated the name instead of translating it, as with all other names. Ish-Qeriot Hebrew name for ‘Iscariot,’ ‘Ish-Qeriot’ means ‘the man from Qeriot.’ Keipha The Aramaic name for ‘Peter,’ and via Greek transliteration: ‘Cephas.’ A number of Aramaic nouns were used in post-exilic Hebrew. Kephar Nachum The Hebrew name for ‘Capernaum.’ Mashiach The Hebrew word for ‘Messiah.’ The Hebrew word which was translated into the Greek ‘Kristos’ but should be clearly distinguished from the Catholic symbolic ‘Christ.’ Literal meaning: “anointed one,” usually referring to one anointed as king of Yisrael, or as priest or prophet. However, when used as a name, it refers to the Son of YHWH, whom he anointed as the ultimate King of Yisrael, the Everlasting High Priest, and the Prophet who would speak YHWH’s words to his people, and if anyone does not obey him, YHWH himself will cut off that person from among his people. See Deu. 18:18. Miryam Hebrew name for ‘Mary’ or ‘Miriam.’ Mosheh Hebrew name for ‘Moses.’ Nephesh (Plural: nephashot.) Lit. “breath,” means ‘life,’ or ‘soul,’ etc. – take note, not the Greek understanding of ‘soul.’ Glossary Naqdimon Hebrew name for ‘Nicodemus.’ Netseret Hebrew name for ‘Nazareth.’ Netanel Hebrew name for ‘Nathanael.’ Perushim ‘Pharisees.’ Pesach Hebrew name for ‘Passover.’ Prophet See Deu. 18: 15-22. When ‘Prophet’ is used as a name, it is a synonym of ‘Mashiach.’ (See glossary: ‘Mashiach’) The use of ‘Prophet’ as a name for Mashiach, should be distinguished from the general use of ‘prophet’ for humans. Context is required to know the difference between ‘Prophet’ and ‘a prophet.’ See e.g. Chapter 1:2 + footnote. Ruach Hebrew word for ‘spirit’ or ‘wind,’ sometimes used with a double meaning. Ruach Ha-Qodesh Lit. “the Set-Apart Spirit.” Shabbat The Hebrew name for ‘Sabbath.’ Shalom Lit. “wholeness,” “completeness” or “well-being,” can also mean ‘peace.’ ‘Shalom’ was also used as a greeting. Shimon Hebrew name for ‘Simon.’ Shomron Hebrew name for ‘Samaria.’ Talmid (Plural: talmidim) Usually translated as “disciples.” The Hebrew ‘Talmid’ is a student who learns from his teacher and follows his example. Tanach ‘Tanach’ is a Hebrew acronym for ‘Torah, Nevi’im, Ketuvim,’ or in English, ‘The Law, the Prophets, the Writings.’ In other words, it refers to the entire so-called ‘Old Testament.’
Luke 1:5	In the days of Herod, king of Judah, [there was] a certain priest whose name was Zechariah, from the priestly division [or: lineage] of Abijah, and his wife was from the daughters [or: descendants] of Aaron, and her name was Elisheva.
Luke 1:6	And both of them were righteous before God, walking [or: standing firm] in the commandments and in the righteous [requirements] of YHWH, without any blemish [or: slander, reproach].
Luke 1:7	And they had no son or daughter, because she was barren, and both of them were very advanced in age.
Luke 1:8	It happened that when Zechariah the priest was carrying out [his] priestly duties before YHWH, in the order [or: appointed sequence] of his division's turn,
Luke 1:9	According to the custom of the priests, he was chosen by lot to offer [or: burn] frankincense, and as he entered into the sanctuary [or: holy place] of YHWH,
Luke 1:10	And all the people were outside, praying at the hour [or: moment] when it was [appointed] for him to offer the frankincense.
Luke 1:11	But a messenger [or: angel] of YHWH, standing at the right side of the altar of frankincense, appeared to him.
Luke 1:12	And when Zechariah saw him, he was terrified [or: alarmed], and fear [or: awe, dread] fell upon him.
Luke 1:13	But the messenger [or: angel] said to him: Do not fear, Zechariah, because your prayer has been heard, and your wife Elisheva will bear you a son, and you shall call his name Yochanan [meaning: YHWH is gracious].
Luke 1:14	And there will be joy and exultation [or: gladness] for you, and many will rejoice when he is born.
Luke 1:15	For he will be great before the Mighty One [or: God], and he will not drink wine or intoxicating drink [or: strong drink]. Before he comes forth from the womb of his mother, he will be filled to completion [or: made whole, perfected] by the spirit of holiness [or: the holy spirit].
Luke 1:17	And he will go [or: be established] before him—that is, before YHWH—in the spirit and in the power [or: vigor] of Elijah, in order that he may turn [or: settle, restore] the heart of the fathers toward the children, and guide them in the wisdom of the righteous, and prepare for YHWH a people made whole [or: complete, at peace].
Luke 1:18	And Zechariah said to the messenger [or: angel], How will this be done? For I am old, and my wife is advanced in her days.
Luke 1:19	And the messenger [or: angel] answered and said to him, I am the one who stands before the Mighty One [or: God], and I have been sent to announce [or: declare, make known] to you these words.
Luke 1:20	Because you did not believe [or: trust] my words, which will be fulfilled [or: completed] in their appointed time, you will be mute [or: speechless] and unable to speak until that day when these words come to their completion [or: fulfillment, end].
Luke 1:21	And the people were there, waiting for Zechariah, and wondering [or: marveling] why he was delayed, standing in the sanctuary [or: holy place].
Luke 1:22	And when he came out from the sanctuary, he was not able to speak to them, and they recognized that he had seen a vision [or: appearance]. By signs he was showing [or: indicating] to them, and he remained mute. The holiness [was] in [the] spirit of God.
Luke 1:23	And when he had completed the days of his service [or: ministry, priestly duty], he went [or: departed] to his house.
Luke 1:24	And after a few days, Elisheba [or: Elizabeth] his wife conceived and hid herself [or: kept herself secluded] [for] five months, saying,
Luke 1:25	This [is what] the Mighty One [or: God] has done for me in those days [when] he looked upon me [or: regarded me], in order to remove my reproach [or: disgrace, shame] from among the women.
Luke 1:26	And in the sixth month, God [the Mighty One] sent the angel [messenger] Gabriel to a city of the Galilee region, whose name [was] Nazareth
Luke 1:27	To a virgin [who was] betrothed [pledged in marriage] to a man whose name [was] Joseph — he [was] from the house [lineage] of David — and the virgin's name [was] Miriam
Luke 1:28	And when the angel [messenger] came in to the virgin, he said to her: YHWH will deliver you [or: save you]! Full of grace [favor, beauty]! YHWH [is] with you! Blessed [are] you among [the] rest of the women!
Luke 1:29	And that virgin, when she heard the words of the angel [messenger], was alarmed [terrified] and was pondering [thinking about] that greeting — how [could] it be
Luke 1:30	And the angel [messenger] said to her: Do not fear, Miriam, for you have found grace [favor, kindness] in the eyes of YHWH
Luke 1:31	Know that you will conceive and give birth to a son, and you shall call his name Yeshua [Salvation]
Luke 1:32	This one will be great and will be called Son of the Most High God [the Supreme Mighty One], and our Lord YHWH will give to him the throne of YHWH, [the] father of his [lineage, or: his Father]
Luke 1:33	And he will reign over the house [lineage] of Jacob forever [in perpetuity], and his kingdom [will have] no end [limit]
Luke 1:34	And Miriam said to the angel [messenger]: How can this be done [come about], since [that] I have no knowledge [intimacy] of a man?
Luke 1:35	And the angel [messenger] answered and said: The Spirit [breath, wind] of Holiness [the Holy Spirit] will come upon you, and the power [vigor, strength] of the Most High will overshadow [shelter over] you. And that which will be born from you will be holy [sacred, set apart], and will be called Son [of God]
Luke 1:36	And look — Elisheva [Elizabeth], your kinswoman, has herself conceived a son in her old age, and this is the sixth month for the one who was called barren. Before you conceive in your womb, [know] that you shall call [him]
Luke 1:37	For no thing [or: matter, word] whatsoever will be impossible before YHWH [the Eternal One]
Luke 1:38	And Miriam [Mary] said: Behold, here [is] the maidservant [or: handmaid, servant-woman] of YHWH [the Eternal One]. Let it be done to me according to your words.
Luke 1:39	And Miriam [Mary] rose up from there in those days and went [or: traveled] in haste [promptly] into the hill country, to a city of Judah.
Luke 1:40	And she entered into the house [or: household] of Zechariah and she gave her a greeting of peace [shalom] to Elisheva [Elizabeth].
Luke 1:41	And immediately when she heard the greeting [shalom] from Miriam [Mary], the child [or: boy] of Elisheva [Elizabeth] leaped with joy in her womb, and Elisheva was filled with [the] Spirit of Holiness [the Holy Spirit].
Luke 1:42	And she cried out [or: exclaimed] with a great [loud] voice and said: Blessed are you among the women, and blessed [is] the fruit of your womb!
Luke 1:43	And how has this come about — that the mother of my lord [or: my master] has come to me?
Luke 1:44	Know [or: you should know] that when your greeting [shalom] reached my ears, the child [or: boy] leaped with great joy [or: exultation] in my womb.
Luke 1:45	And blessed [or: happy] are you who believed [trusted], for indeed [or: and did not doubt, because] those words that were spoken to you from YHWH [the Eternal One] will be fulfilled [completed, brought to perfection] in you.
Luke 1:46	And Miriam declared: My innermost being [my soul, my breath-of-life] praises and exalts [lifts up] God
Luke 1:47	And my spirit rejoices [is filled with gladness] in God, who is my salvation [my wholeness, my peace]
Luke 1:48	For he gazed upon the lowliness [the humble state] of his maidservant, and because of this all creatures will declare me to be blessed [radiant, illuminated]
Luke 1:49	For the one who is wholly [entirely] able — the Holy One is his name — he has done in me [for me] great things
Luke 1:50	His steadfast lovingkindness [mercy, covenant-faithfulness] [endures] from generation to generation for those who revere [fear] him
Luke 1:51	For he performs mighty [powerful, forceful] deeds with his arm, and he scorns [treats with contempt] the arrogant in the scheming [imagination] of their heart
Luke 1:52	And he brings down [humbles] the mighty and the lofty from their thrones [seats of power], and lifts up the lowly [humble, downtrodden]
Luke 1:53	The hungry he fills [satisfies] with every good thing, and sends away the wealthy empty [empty-handed, with nothing]
Luke 1:54	He redeems [rescues, ransoms] Israel his servant-son [his child], remembering his steadfast lovingkindnesses [covenant mercies]
Luke 1:55	Just as he promised [vowed] to our ancestors, to Abraham and to his seed [descendants] for all ages [forever]
Luke 1:56	And Miriam [or: Mary] took up her dwelling with Elisheba [Elizabeth] for close to three months, and afterward she returned to her home [which was] in the sanctuary [holy place, temple]
Luke 1:57	Elisheba's [Elizabeth's] time came for her to give birth, and she gave birth, and she had a son
Luke 1:58	And [her] neighbors and her relatives heard of that exalted loving-kindness [grace, covenant-faithfulness] of hers, and they rejoiced with her together [as one]
Luke 1:59	They heard and gathered [together] on the eighth day, in order that they would circumcise the son, and they called him [by the] name of his father, Zechariah
Luke 1:60	And his mother answered and said, No, it shall not be so — rather [but], his name shall be Yochanan [John, meaning: YHWH is gracious]
Luke 1:61	And they said to her, There is no [one] fitting [or: worthy] in your family that would have such a name [as this]
Luke 1:62	And they made a sign [gesture] to his father [asking] how he desired [wished, was pleased] his name would be
Luke 1:63	And Zechariah asked for a single tablet [writing board], on which he wrote: 'Yochanan — that is his name.' And all who were there were greatly astonished [amazed] at this
Luke 1:64	And immediately the mouth of the father was opened, and his tongue spoke [again], and he blessed [praised] YHWH
Luke 1:65	And fear [awe, dread] came upon all their neighbors, and throughout all the hill country of Judah these words [matters] were spread [reported]
Luke 1:66	And all who heard [these things] stored them away in their hearts, and said, 'What then will this child become?' For the hand [or: power] of YHWH was upon him.
Luke 1:67	And Zechariah his father was filled [with the] spirit of holiness [or: the holy spirit], and he prophesied and said:
Luke 1:68	Blessed be [the] Lord, [the] God of Israel, for he has visited [or: attended to] and accomplished redemption [or: ransom] for his people.
Luke 1:69	And he directed [or: made straight the way for] us and swiftly gave [or: hastened to grant] deliverance [or: peace, wholeness] from [the] house of David his servant.
Luke 1:70	Just as he declared through [or: from] the mouth of his holy ones, the prophets, who [have spoken] from the beginning of the age [or: from of old, from time immemorial].
Luke 1:71	Deliverance [or: peace, salvation] for us from those who hate us, and from the hand [or: power, grip] of all those who provoked us [or: stirred us to wrath].
Luke 1:72	To show [or: do, perform] faithful love [or: kindness, covenant loyalty] with our fathers, and to remember his holy covenant [or: the holiness of his charge/command].
Luke 1:73	And the oath that he swore to Abraham our father — [it] will be granted [or: fulfilled, given] to us.
Luke 1:74	So that he would rescue us from our enemies, [and] without fear [or: dread] we would serve [or: worship] him.
Luke 1:75	In holiness [or: consecration] and in purity [or: cleanness] before him all our days.
Luke 1:76	And you, child, will be called a prophet of the Most High [or: supremely exalted], for you will walk before the Lord [the Sovereign One] in order to prepare the ways so that they will be directed [toward] wholeness [or: peace, well-being] — you will fulfill his service, and [bring] peace in the way of peace and in the way of God [the Mighty One]
Luke 1:77	And to give complete [or: full, whole] healing to his people for the forgiveness [or: pardon] of their iniquities [or: sins, transgressions]
Luke 1:78	On account of his mercies [or: lovingkindnesses] by which he visits [or: attends to] us — he who is [from on] high [or: the exalted one]
Luke 1:79	And he shone light upon [or: illuminated] those who are dwelling in deep darkness [or: thick gloom] and in [the] shadow of death, in order that our feet will be guided into light on the way [or: path] of peace [or: wholeness, well-being]
Luke 1:80	And the child was growing and becoming strong [or: strengthened] in the spirit of holiness [the Holy Spirit], and [was] dwelling in the wilderness places until the time that he revealed [or: showed] himself to Israel

Luke 2:43	And when the days [of the feast] were completed, as they returned [turned back], the boy [youth] remained in Jerusalem, and his father and his mother did not notice [recognize, perceive] him [missing]
Luke 2:44	And [they were] thinking [assuming, supposing] that he would be with the others who were going [traveling] with them, and so they traveled one day's [journey], and they searched for him among the rest [remainder] of the relatives [and] the acquaintances [known ones, familiar persons]

Luke 5:3	And he went up into one of the boats, which belonged to Shimon, and asked [or: requested] him to push the boat out from the water [or: shore] a little. Then he sat down and [began] teaching the people [or: crowd] from the boat

Luke 5:5	And Shimon answered and said to him: All [through] the night we have toiled [or: labored] and we have not caught a thing [or: anything]. But at your word, we will cast out [or: extend] your nets
Luke 5:6	And when this was done, they gathered [or: collected] a great [quantity of] fish — so many that their nets were breaking [or: tearing apart]

Luke 5:20	And when Yeshua saw their faith [or: faithfulness, firmness of trust], he said: Man of faith — your iniquities [or: transgressions] are forgiven [or: pardoned].

Luke 5:28	And the man rose and left [or: set aside] all his affairs [or: matters], and walked after him.

Luke 5:37	And no person has [the right] to put new wine into an old wineskin, for if he does so, the new wine bursts [or: breaks] the old wineskins, and the skins are destroyed and the wine is spilled out.
Luke 5:38	But new wine is fitting [or: proper] to put into a new wineskin, and [both] the wine and the skins are preserved [or: sustained, endure together].
Luke 5:39	And no person [who is] drinking old wine wants immediately to drink new wine, for he says: The old — it is good [or: better, pleasant].

Luke 6:23	Rejoice in that day and be lifted up [or: exalted], for your reward is exceedingly abundant [or: poured out] in the heavens. In the same way they were doing [these things] to the prophets and to the fathers [or: ancestors].

Luke 6:27	But I say to you who are hearing [this]: Love your enemies and [love] those who [hate] you, and do good to whoever has provoked [or: angered] you.
Luke 6:28	Bless whoever says evil [or: harmful things] to you. Pray on behalf of those who oppress [or: burden, dishonor] you.
Luke 6:29	And to the one who strikes you on the one cheek, offer [or: prepare] for him the other. And the one who desires to rob [or: seize] your garment, do not wish to withhold [it] from him — and also the cloak, if he desires to seize it.
Luke 6:30	But [or: truly] to every person who asks you, give. And from the one who robs what is yours, do not seek [it] back [or: do not demand them back].
Luke 6:31	And just as you desire [or: delight in, wish] that people would do for you, do [the same] for them
Luke 6:32	If you love those who love you, what kind of grace [or: favor, kindness] is fitting for you? [It is] truth [or: certain] that sinners do likewise
Luke 6:34	And if you give to those from whom you hope [to receive] a reward [or: payment, wages] or service, what kind of grace [or: favor] is fitting for you? For sinners give to sinners in order that they may receive recompense [or: requital]
Luke 6:35	But [or: rather, truly] love your enemies and do good to them and give gifts to those from whom you do not expect a thing, and your reward [or: wages, compensation] will be exceedingly great, and you will be children of God Most High [or: the Supreme], for he is exceedingly good toward the ungrateful [or: those who provoke goodwill] and toward the evil [or: wicked]
Luke 6:36	Therefore be possessors of compassion [or: graciousness, mercy], just as your father is gracious [or: compassionate]
Luke 6:37	Do not desire [or: delight in] judging, and you will not be judged. Do not desire [or: delight in] punishing, and you will not be punished. Release [or: let go, forgive], and it will be released [or: forgiven] to you
Luke 6:38	Give, and I will give to you a measure [that is] good [or: beneficial] and tested [or: proven, examined] and just [or: righteous]. Give, and an even more exalted [measure] will be given to you in the end [or: at the conclusion], for in that same measure that you measure out, it will be measured to you, and they will give to you according to its likeness [or: in proportion to it]
Luke 6:39	It is not possible [for] a blind one to lead another blind one; [it is] a certainty [or: necessity] that both of them will fall into a pit [or: cistern]
Luke 6:40	A pupil is not above the master [or: the great one]. Every pupil is complete [or: perfected, made whole] when he is like his master [or: teacher]
Luke 6:41	How [is it that] you see a splinter [or: sliver] in the eye of your brother, yet you do not consider that you yourself carry in yours a great beam [or: bar, plank]
Luke 6:42	And how can you say to your brother 'Allow me and I will remove the splinter [or: thorn] from your eye' — you deceiver! Drive out [or: expel] first the beam [or: bar, bolt] from your own eye, and then you will see clearly to drive out the splinter from the eye of your brother.
Luke 6:43	For the tree [that] is not good produces bad [or: evil, rotten] fruit, and a bad [or: evil, corrupt] tree is not able to produce good fruit.
Luke 6:44	For every tree is recognized [or: known, distinguished] by its fruit — for from thorns no one gathers figs, and from a bramble [or: thorn-bush] no one gathers grapes.
Luke 6:45	A good person, from the goodness [or: good store] of the treasury of his heart, brings forth [or: declares, tells] good; and an evil [or: bad, corrupt] person, from the evil [or: wickedness] of the treasury of his heart, brings forth evil — for from the overflow [or: abundance] of the heart, the mouth speaks.
Luke 6:46	Why do you call me 'Lord' [or: master, sovereign] yet you are not doing what I command [or: enjoin, charge]?
Luke 6:47	Every person who comes to me and hears [or: listens to] my words and does [or: practices, carries out] them — I will show you to whom he is comparable [or: resembles].
Luke 6:48	He is comparable to a person who builds a house, who digs deep [and] sets the foundation upon the rock [or: stone]. And when a great surge [or: flood] of water came — the torrent struck against that house and was not able to shake it, for it had been founded [or: firmly established] upon the rock.
Luke 6:49	But whoever hears my words and does not put them into practice [or: action] — he is comparable to a person building a house upon the ground without a foundation. And the water from the torrent came and struck it, and immediately it fell, and the ruin [or: destruction, devastation] of that house was great.

Luke 9:4	And into whatever house you enter, there you shall stay, and from there you shall not go out.

Luke 9:6	And when you go out from there, go through [or: search out] the houses, preaching [or: seeking] and healing the sick in every place.

Luke 9:17	And they all ate and were satisfied, and the remainder [or: leftover] was twelve baskets full.

Luke 9:19	And they answered and said, 'Yochanan [John] the Immerser [or: Baptizer].' And some say, 'Eliyahu [Elijah].' And there are [those] saying, 'A prophet from the ancients [or: former ones] who has returned alive.' And the twelve approached.

Luke 10:1	And afterward, Yeshua revealed [himself] to the other seventy-three disciples, and sent them two [by] two ahead of him into every place that he was about [or: prepared, destined] to come.

Luke 10:3	Go! Look — I [am] sending you out like lambs [or: young sheep] among wolves. Give to us our allotted bread [or: food, sustenance] day [by] day.
Luke 10:4	Do not carry a sack or a [money]stone [or: purse], nor leg-coverings [or: stockings], nor sandals, and do not give a greeting [or: peace, shalom] to any person on the road. And forgive [or: release, let go] to us all our sins, for we [are] forgiving [or: releasing] them to all who are indebted to us, and do not bring us into trial [or: testing, temptation].
Luke 10:5	And in whatever house you enter, you shall say first: Peace [or: wholeness, well-being] will come upon this house. And he said to them: Which among you has a friend, and goes to him at midnight, and says to him: My beloved [or: dear friend], lend me three loaves of bread.

Luke 10:20	But [or: However, only] do not rejoice that they are subjected to you; rather [rejoice] that your name is written [or: inscribed] in the heavens.

Luke 10:23	And he turned his face toward his disciples and said, Blessed [or: fortunate, happy] are those who see what you are seeing.

Luke 10:26	And he said to him, What is written in the law concerning this matter [or: regarding this subject]?
Luke 10:27	And he answered and said: You shall love the Lord [or: Sovereign, Master] your God with all [the fullness of] your heart, and with all your being [or: soul, life-breath], and with all your strength [or: power], and your thought [or: mind, intention] — and you shall love your neighbor [or: companion, fellow] as yourself

Luke 10:31	And it happened that a certain priest [was] walking on that same road, and he saw him and passed on [to the far side]
Luke 10:32	And a certain Levite [came] near to that place, and behold he [too was] passing from [there] and onward [or: beyond]
Luke 10:33	But a certain Samaritan, passing near him, came to him and saw him and had compassion [or: showed mercy, pitied] upon him
Luke 10:34	And he applied [or: poured] oil on his wounds, and bound them up, and brought him to the inn [ushpiza], and set [or: kept] his eyes upon him [watching over him attentively]
Luke 10:35	And on the next day, he took two peshitin [coins] and gave them to the innkeeper, and said to him: Provide for this wounded man all [of] his needs. And if you spend on his behalf more than these peshitin [coins], I will give [the remainder] to you upon my return.
Luke 10:36	Which one of these three seems to you to have been more [of] a companion [or: neighbor, associate] and friend [or: fellow] to the one who fell among the robbers?
Luke 10:37	And he said to him: The one who showed [or: did] him the faithful-kindness [chesed]. And Yeshua said to him: Go, and you do likewise.
Luke 10:38	And it happened that while [they were] walking, he entered a certain village, and a woman whose name was Miriam [Mary] received him into her home. And the Pharisee said in his heart: Why does this [man] not wash his hands at the morning meal?

And our lord said to him: Pharisees! Now you purify what is on the outside of the cup and the bowl, but what is on the inside of you — it is an evil [or: wicked] heart and theft [or: robbery].

And he said to his disciples: It is an impossible thing that troubles [or: stumbling blocks] will not come, but woe to the one through whom they come.

Luke 20:4	The immersion [baptism] of Yochanan — was it from the heavens [or: from the divine realm] or from men?
Luke 20:5	And they were deliberating [or: reasoning] among themselves, saying: If we say 'from the heavens,' he will say to us, 'If so, [why] did you not believe in him?'
Luke 20:6	And if we say 'from men,' all the people will stone us, because it was [a matter of] certainty [or: clarity] to them that Yochanan was a prophet
Luke 20:7	And they answered that they did not know from where it [had] come [or: from where it was]
Luke 20:8	And Yeshua said to them, Neither am I telling you by what authority [or: rule, dominion] I am doing these things
Luke 20:9	And he began to say to the people this parable [or: matter]: A certain man planted a vineyard and rented it out to workers of [the] land [or: soil], and he was not there [for] a long time
Luke 20:10	And at the time of the vintage [or: grape harvest] he sent his servant to those holding [or: managing] the vineyard so that they would give him [his share] of the fruit, but they struck [or: beat] his servant and did not give him anything
Luke 20:11	And the sovereign [master, lord] sent another servant, and they oppressed [drove, exacted from] him and broke [shattered] him with an abundance of humiliations [contemptuous acts], and they let him go [sent him away]
Luke 20:12	And he sent a third servant, and [he was] struck [beaten] with wounds [bruises, welts], [and] they sent him away outside
Luke 20:13	And the sovereign [master] of the vineyard said, What shall I do? I will send my precious [dear, costly] son, the beloved [one] — perhaps [or: if only] when they see him they will be ashamed [abashed, put to shame] before him
Luke 20:14	And when the holders [tenants, those who seized] the vineyard saw him, in their cunning schemes [treacherous plots] they said, This is the heir — come, let us kill him, and the inheritance will be ours
Luke 20:15	And they cast [threw] him out from the vineyard and killed him. Therefore, [the] owner [master] of the vineyard — what will he do to them, the vintage-gatherers [or: the fortified ones]?
Luke 20:16	He will come and destroy those workers [tillers] of the ground [soil]. And [when] the matter was heard, they said, YHWH would never consent [to such a thing]! [or: May it not be so, YHWH!]
Luke 20:17	If so, what does this that is written intend to say: A stone [that] the builders rejected — it has become placed [set, laid] at the head [top] of the corner
Luke 20:18	Every person who falls upon that stone will be struck [shattered, broken] by the stone, and the stone will crush [grind to powder] those upon whom it falls
Luke 20:19	And the chiefs [officers, princes] of the priests and the scribes desired in that [very] hour to lay a hand on him, but they feared the people, for they recognized that it was about them and on their account [that] he was speaking the parable [allegory, figurative saying]
Luke 20:20	And they sent to him men who make [present] themselves [as] righteous [just, pious], in order that they might catch him in some word [matter, statement] and deliver him to the rulers [authorities, officials]
Luke 20:21	And they questioned him, saying: Teacher, we know that you speak and teach righteous [or: just, upright] things and do not fear [or: shrink back] before any person, but only speak the truth and teach the way of God [the Mighty One].
Luke 20:22	Are we obligated [or: liable, bound] to give tax [or: tribute, forced levy] to Caesar, or not?
Luke 20:23	And he, discerning [or: considering, reckoning] the deceit [or: falsehood] of their lies, said to them: Why are you testing [or: putting to the test] me?
Luke 20:24	Show me one coin [or: simple coin]. Whose image [or: likeness] and inscription [or: writing] is on it? And they answered him: [It is] of Caesar.
Luke 20:25	And he said to them: If [that is] so, [then] all the things that belong to [or: are from] Caesar, give [or: render, return] to Caesar; and those that belong to [or: are from] YHWH, give [or: render, return] to YHWH.
Luke 20:26	And they were not able to respond [or: answer, reply] to his words before [or: in the presence of] the people, and being astonished [or: marveling] at his answer [or: response], they fell silent.
Luke 20:27	And some [or: a portion] of the Sadducees — who did not acknowledge [or: confess, admit] the resurrection of the dead — drew near to him and questioned him.
Luke 20:28	Teacher, Moses wrote [or: prescribed, inscribed] for us: [If] a man who has a wife dies without children [or: sons], his brother should [or: is able to] take her as a wife to raise up [or: establish] offspring [or: seed, posterity] for his brother.
